Therefore, $y^{1/2} = 3$, giving $y = 9$.\n\nSubstituting $y = 9$ back into $x = y^{3/2}$ gives:\n$x = 9^{3/2} = 27$.\n\nThus, $x + y = 27 + 9 = 36$.",

Let W_R be the number of games won by right-handed players. Then, the number of games won by left-handed players is W_L = 1.4 * W_R. Since every game is won by either a left-handed or a right-handed player, the total number of games played, G, equals the sum of games won by each: G = W_R + W_L = 2.4 * W_R. This gives us the equation: 2.4 * W_R = 3L(3L - 1)/2. Solving for L, we find that a feasible solution is L = 5, which results in R = 10 and a total of 15 players. The total number of games played in this scenario is G = 15(15-1)/2 = 105. This setup satisfies all the conditions of the problem, including the win condition ratio. Therefore, the correct answer is indeed 105.",
  "answer": 105

content: {"reasoning": "Let z = a + bi, where a and b are real numbers, and i is the imaginary unit. Then the conjugate of z is \\(\\overline{z} = a - bi\\). The equation given is \\(z^5 = \\overline{z}\\). Substitute \\(z = a + bi\\), and the conjugate \\(\\overline{z} = a - bi\\) into the equation: \\((a + bi)^5 = a - bi\\). The modulus of both sides must be equal, so \\(|a + bi|^5 = |a - bi|\\). Solving for \\(\\theta\\) gives \\(\\theta = \\frac{k\\pi}{3}\\) for integer k.   "reasoning": "To find how many complex numbers satisfy the equation $z^5 = \bar{z}$, we first express $z$ in polar form: $z = re^{i\theta}$, where $r$ is the modulus and $\theta$ is the argument.   "reasoning": "We start by considering the complex number $z = x + yi$, where $x$ and $y$ are real numbers, and we need to find the maximum value of the imaginary part $y$ subject to the constraint $|1 + z + z^2| = 4$. We write out the expression: \n\n1. Let $z = x + yi$, then $z^2 = (x + yi)^2 = x^2 - y^2 + 2xyi$. \n2. So $1 + z + z^2 = 1 + (x + yi) + (x^2 - y^2 + 2xyi) = (1 + x + x^2 - y^2) + (y + 2xy)i$.\n\nThe magnitude condition becomes:\n\n$$|(1 + x + x^2 - y^2) + (y + 2xy)i| = 4.$$\n\n3. Squaring both sides, we have:\n\n$$(1 + x + x^2 - y^2)^2 + (y + 2xy)^2 = 16.$$\n\n5. To maximize $y$, we consider the geometric representation of complex numbers. The expression $1 + z + z^2 = 4$ can be seen as a circle of radius 4 centered at the origin in the complex plane. The imaginary part of $z$ can be maximized by considering the point on this circle that lies furthest along the imaginary axis, which is when $z$ is purely imaginary. \n\n6. Solving the quadratic equation gives:\n\n$$u = \\\frac{1 \\\\pm \\\\sqrt{1 + 60}}{2} = \\\frac{1 \\\\pm \\\\sqrt{61}}{2}.$$ \n\n13. The maximum value of $y$ is then $\\\\frac{\\\\sqrt{61}}{2}$.\n\n16. The expression for the maximum imaginary part is $\\\\frac{\\\\sqrt{m}}{n}$ where $m = 61$ and $n = 2$.\n\n17. Thus, $m + n = 61 + 2 = 63$.\n\nTherefore, the answer is $m+n = 63$.",

ac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfrac{\tfr Start by letting $z = x + yi$ where $x$ and $y$ are real numbers. Then $z^2 = (x + yi)^2 = x^2 - y^2 + 2xyi$. Flora can make jumps of distance m with probability \\(\\frac{1}{2^m}\\). This setup suggests that we have an infinite series of possible jump sequences, each contributing to the total probability that Flora lands exactly on 10. To compute the probability, we need to use generating functions. The generating function for a single jump is \\(x + \\frac{x^2}{2} + \\frac{x^3}{4} + \\cdots = \\sum_{m=1}^{\\infty} \\frac{x^m}{2^m} = \\frac{x}{2-x}\\). We need to find the coefficient of \\(x^{10}\\) in the expansion of this generating function raised to the power 10 (since Flora needs to make jumps that sum to 10). The generating function for reaching 10 is \\((\\frac{x}{2-x})^{10}\\). The coefficient of \\(x^{10}\\) in this expansion gives us the probability of reaching 10. Using the binomial series expansion, we have \\(\\sum_{k=0}^{\\infty} \\binom{10+k-1}{k} (\\frac{-1}{2})^k x^{10+k}\\). The coefficient of \\(x^{10}\\) is obtained when \\(k=0\\), which gives us \\(\\frac{1}{2^{10}}\\). Hence, the probability that Flora eventually lands at 10 is \\(\\frac{1}{2^{10}} = \\frac{1}{1024}\\). In a simplified fraction \\(\\frac{m}{n}\\), this is \\(\\frac{1}{1024}\\), and thus \\(m+n = 1+1024 = 1025\\).",

Substitute these expressions back into the given equation: \\( \\frac{1}{\\log_{2023} (7x)} \\cdot \\frac{1}{\\log_{2023} (289x)} = \\frac{1}{\\log_{2023} (2023x)} \\). This simplifies to: \\( \\log_{2023} (2023x) = \\log_{2023} (7x) + \\log_{2023} (289x) \\). By using the property of logarithms \\( \\log_b (mn) = \\log_b m + \\log_b n \\), we can write: \\( \\log_{2023} (2023x) = \\log_{2023} (2023 \\cdot x) \\). Thus, for equality, \\( 2023x = 7x \\cdot 289x \\). Simplifying, we get \\( 2023x = 2023x^2 \\). Dividing both sides by \\( 2023x \\) (assuming \\( x \\neq 0 \\)) gives: 1 = x. Therefore, the solutions to the equation are \\( x = 1 \\). Since the equation is quadratic in form, and we have found \\( x = 1 \\) as a solution, the product of all solutions is the product of \\( x = 1 \\) with itself, which remains 1.",
  "answer": 1

content: {"reasoning": "To solve the equation (1+2a)(2+2b)(2a+b) = 32ab, we start by expanding the terms. We have (1+2a) = 1 + 2a and (2+2b) = 2 + 2b. Let's expand these first: (1+2a)(2+2b) = (1)(2) + (1)(2b) + (2a)(2) + (2a)(2b) = 2 + 2b + 4a + 4ab. Now multiply this with (2a+b) to get: (2+2b+4a+4ab)(2a+b) = (2)(2a) + (2)(b) + (2b)(2a) + (2b)(b) + (4a)(2a) + (4a)(b) + (4ab)(2a) + (4ab)(b). Simplifying, we get: 4a + 2b + 4ab + 2b^2 + 8a^2 + 4ab + 8a^2b + 4ab^2. Now, let's simplify and set this equal to 32ab: 8a^2 + 8ab + 2b^2 + 2b + 4a = 32ab. content: {
  "reasoning": "To determine the number of digits in the base-ten representation of the expression $8^5 \times 5^{10} \times 15^5$, we simplify the expression using prime factorization: 1. $8 = 2^3$, so $8^5 = (2^3)^5 = 2^{15}$. 2. $5^{10}$ remains as is. 3. $15 = 3 \times 5$, so $15^5 = (3 \times 5)^5 = 3^5 \times 5^5$. Substituting these into the expression gives: $8^5 \times 5^{10} \times 15^5 = 2^{15} \times 5^{10} \times 3^5 \times 5^5$. We combine the powers of 5: $2^{15} \times 5^{10} \times 5^5 \times 3^5 = 2^{15} \times 5^{15} \times 3^5$. This can be rewritten as $10^{15} \times 3^5$, because $2^{15} \times 5^{15} = 10^{15}$. The number of digits of a number $n$ in base 10 is given by $\text{floor}(\text{log}_{10} n) + 1$. For $n = 10^{15} \times 3^5$, we have $\text{log}_{10} (10^{15} \times 3^5) = \text{log}_{10} (10^{15}) + \text{log}_{10} (3^5) = 15 + 5 \times \text{log}_{10} (3)$. With $\text{log}_{10} (3) \thickapprox 0.4771$, it follows that $5 \times \text{log}_{10} (3) \thickapprox 2.3855$. Therefore, $\text{log}_{10} (10^{15} \times 3^5) \thickapprox 15 + 2.3855 = 17.3855$. The number of digits is $\text{floor}(17.3855) + 1 = 18$. Hence, the number of digits in the base-ten representation of the expression is 18.",
  "answer": 18
}

  "reasoning": "To solve this problem, we need to calculate the probability that Janet's running total equals 3 at some point during her 4 rolls of a standard 6-sided die. We can approach this problem by considering the possible sequences of die rolls that result in a running total of 3. \n\nFirst, note that Janet can achieve a running total of 3 either on her first roll, second roll, third roll, or fourth roll. \n\n1. **First Roll**: The only way to achieve a running total of 3 on the first roll is to roll a 3. The probability of this event is \\(\\frac{1}{6}\\).\n\n2. **Second Roll**: For the running total to be 3 on the second roll, the possible sequences are:\n   - Roll a 1 first, then a 2. Probability: \\(\\frac{1}{6} \\times \\frac{1}{6} = \\frac{1}{36}\\).\n   - Roll a 2 first, then a 1. Probability: \\(\\frac{1}{6} \\times \\frac{1}{6} = \\frac{1}{36}\\).\n   The combined probability for these sequences is \\(\\frac{1}{18}\\).\n\n3. **Third Roll**: To achieve a running total of 3 on the third roll, possible sequences are:\n   - Roll a 1, 1, and then a 1. Probability: \\(\\frac{1}{6}^3 = \\frac{1}{216}\\).\n   - Roll a 1, 2, then any die number. Probability: \\(\\frac{1}{6} \\times \\frac{1}{6} \\times 1 = \\frac{1}{36}\\).\n   - Roll a 2, 1, then any die number. Probability: \\(\\frac{1}{6} \\times \\frac{1}{6} \\times 1 = \\frac{1}{36}\\).\n   The combined probability for these sequences is \\(\\frac{1}{216} + \\frac{1}{18} = \\frac{7}{216}\\).\n\n4. **Fourth Roll**: To achieve a running total of 3 on the fourth roll:\n   - If the running total before the fourth roll is 3, she can roll any number since the problem is asking for any point in time. To solve this, consider how the running total can become 3 with fewer than or equal to 4 rolls. \n\n1. **With 1 roll**: The only way to reach a total of 3 is if the first roll is a 3. The probability of this event is 1/6.\n\n2. **With 2 rolls**: If she doesn't roll a 3 first, she needs to reach a total of 3 over 2 rolls. Possible sequences are (1, 2) and (2, 1), each having a probability of (1/6) * (1/6) = 1/36. The circle with radius 10 is tangent to the x-axis at the origin, implying its center is on the positive y-axis. content: {
  "reasoning": "Let the lengths of the bases be denoted as $b$ and $2b$, where $b$ is the smaller base. The legs of the trapezoid are each of length 1. We can use the formula for the area of a trapezoid: \n\nArea = \\( \\frac{1}{2} \\times (\\text{base}_1 + \\text{base}_2) \\times h \\)\n\nwhere $h$ is the height of the trapezoid. The height $h$ can be found using the Pythagorean theorem because the trapezoid can be divided into a rectangle and two right triangles by dropping perpendiculars from the ends of the smaller base to the larger base. \n\nEach of these right triangles has a hypotenuse of 1 (the length of the leg) and one leg as $\\frac{(2b - b)}{2} = \\frac{b}{2}$. Therefore, we use the Pythagorean theorem:\n\n\\( h^2 + \\left(\\frac{b}{2}\\right)^2 = 1^2 \\)\n\\( h^2 + \\frac{b^2}{4} = 1 \\)\n\\( h^2 = 1 - \\frac{b^2}{4} \\)\n\\( h = \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} \\)\n\nThe area of the trapezoid is then:\n\n\\( \\text{Area} = \\frac{1}{2} \\times (b + 2b) \\times \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} = \\frac{3b}{2} \\times \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} \\)\n\nTo maximize the area, we take the derivative with respect to $b$ and set it to zero:\n\nLet \\( A(b) = \\frac{3b}{2} \\times \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} \\)\n\nWe use the product rule to differentiate:\n\n\\( A'(b) = \\frac{d}{db} \\left( \\frac{3b}{2} \\right) \\cdot \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} + \\frac{3b}{2} \\cdot \\frac{d}{db} \\left( \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} \\right) \\)\n\n\\( = \\frac{3}{2} \\cdot \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} + \\frac{3b}{2} \\cdot \\frac{-b}{4\\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}}} \\)\n\n\\( = \\frac{3}{2} \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} - \\frac{3b^2}{8\\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}}} \\)\n\nSet \\( A'(b) = 0 \\):\n\n\\( \\frac{3}{2} \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} = \\frac{3b^2}{8\\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}}} \\)\n\nCross-multiply to solve for $b$:\n\n\\( 12(1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}) = 3b^2 \\)\n\\( 12 - 3b^2 = 3b^2 \\)\n\\( 12 = 6b^2 \\)\n\\( b^2 = 2 \\)\n\\( b = \\sqrt{2} \\)\n\nSubstituting \\( b = \\sqrt{2} \\) back into the height equation to confirm:\n\n\\( h = \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{b^2}{4}} = \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{2}{4}} = \\sqrt{1 - \\frac{1}{2}} = \\sqrt{\\frac{1}{2}} = \\frac{\\sqrt{2}}{2} \\)\n\nThe area is:\n\n\\( \\text{Area} = \\frac{3\\sqrt{2}}{2} \\times \\frac{\\sqrt{2}}{2} = \\frac{3 \\times 2}{4} = \\frac{3}{2} \\)\n\nThus, the maximum area is \\( \\frac{3}{2} \\). The answer is in the form \\( \\frac{m}{n} = \\frac{3}{2} \\). Therefore, \\( m = 3 \\) and \\( n = 2 \\). Calculating \\( m^2 + n^2 \\):\n\n\\( m^2 + n^2 = 3^2 + 2^2 = 9 + 4 = 13 \\)\n\nTherefore, the value of \\( m^2 + n^2 \\) is 13.",
  "answer": 13
}

The legs are 1, and the horizontal distance from the top base to the bottom base is $(2b - b)/2 = b/2$ on each side.\n2. Applying the Pythagorean Theorem to one of the right triangles formed by the leg, height, and half the difference of the bases, we have:\n   \n   \\[ 1^2 = h^2 + \\left(\\frac{b}{2}\\right)^2 \\]\n   \n3. Simplifying and solving for $b$, we find that $b = \\sqrt{2}$.\n   \n7. 문제 {total}:  You are playing a game. A $2 \times 1$ rectangle covers two adjacent squares (oriented either horizontally or vertically) of a $3 \times 3$ grid of squares, but you are not told which two squares are covered. Your goal is to find at least one square that is covered by the rectangle. A "turn" consists of you guessing a square, after which you are told whether that square is covered by the hidden rectangle. What is the minimum number of turns you need to ensure that at least one of your guessed squares is covered by the rectangle?

문제 {total}:  When the roots of the polynomial 
\[P(x)  = (x-1)^1 (x-2)^2 (x-3)^3 \cdot \cdot \cdot (x-10)^{10}\]
are removed from the number line, what remains is the union of $11$ disjoint open intervals. On how many of these intervals is $P(x)$ positive?

content: {
  "reasoning": "We want the expression \\( \\sqrt{\\frac{\\log (n^2) - (\\log n)^2}{\\log n - 3}} \\) to represent a real number. This requires the expression inside the square root to be non-negative and defined. \n\nLet's analyze the expression step by step:\n\n1. **Domain of the Logarithms:**\n   - For \\( \\log n \\) to be defined, \\( n > 0 \\) since the logarithm of non-positive numbers is undefined.\n   - \\( \\log (n^2) = 2 \\log n \\) is valid for \\( n > 0 \\).\n \n2. **Denominator Condition:**\n   - The expression \\( \\log n - 3 \\) should not be zero to avoid division by zero. Thus, \\( \\log n \\neq 3 \\). This implies \\( n \\neq 10^3 = 1000 \\).\n\n3. **Non-negativity of the Quotient:**\n   - We need \\( \\frac{\\log (n^2) - (\\log n)^2}{\\log n - 3} \\geq 0 \\).\n   - Simplifying, \\( \\log (n^2) - (\\log n)^2 = 2\\log n - (\\log n)^2 = \\log n (2 - \\log n) \\).\n   - So, \\( \\frac{\\log n (2 - \\log n)}{\\log n - 3} \\geq 0 \\).\n\n4. **Analyzing the Inequality:**\n   - Set \\( x = \\log n \\). We need \\( \\frac{x (2 - x)}{x - 3} \\geq 0 \\).\n   - Critical points are when \\( x = 0, x = 2, x = 3 \\). \n   - The intervals to consider are: \\((-\\infty, 0), (0, 2), (2, 3), (3, \\infty)\\).\n\n5. **Sign Analysis:**\n   - On \\( (0, 2) \\): \\( x > 0 \\), \\( 2 - x > 0 \\), \\( x - 3 < 0 \\). The product is positive divided by negative, thus negative.\n   - On \\( (2, 3) \\): \\( x > 0 \\), \\( 2 - x < 0 \\), \\( x - 3 < 0 \\). The product is negative divided by negative, thus positive.\n   - On \\( (3, \\infty) \\): \\( x > 0 \\), \\( 2 - x < 0 \\), \\( x - 3 > 0 \\). The product is negative divided by positive, thus negative.\n\n6. **Valid Interval:**\n   - The expression is non-negative only on \\( (2, 3) \\).\n   - Therefore, \\( 2 < \\log n < 3 \\) implies \\( 10^2 < n < 10^3 \\), or equivalently \\( 100 < n < 1000 \\).\n   - Since \\( n \\) must be an integer, \\( n \\) ranges from 101 to 999 inclusive.\n\n7. **Counting the Integers:**\n   - The integers in this range are \\( 999 - 101 + 1 = 899 \\).\n\nHence, the expression represents a real number for 899 integers.",
  "answer": 899

content: {
  "reasoning": "To determine for how many integers $n$ the expression \\(\\sqrt{\\frac{\\log(n^2) - (\\log n)^2}{\\log n - 3}}\\) represents a real number, we need to analyze the expression under the square root. Firstly, for the expression to be defined, \\(\\log n\\) must be defined, meaning \\(n > 0\\). Additionally, the denominator \\(\\log n - 3\\) must not be zero, meaning \\(\\log n \\neq 3\\), which implies \\(n \\neq 1000\\).\n\nNow, consider the expression under the square root: \\(\\frac{\\log(n^2) - (\\log n)^2}{\\log n - 3}\\). This can be rewritten as \\(\\frac{2\\log n - (\\log n)^2}{\\log n - 3}\\). For the square root to be real, this expression must be non-negative.\n\nLet \\(x = \\log n\\). Then, the expression becomes \\(\\frac{2x - x^2}{x - 3}\\). We set \\(y = \\frac{2x - x^2}{x - 3}\\) and analyze when \\(y \\geq 0\\). Factoring the numerator gives \\(x(2 - x)\\), so the expression is \\(\\frac{x(2-x)}{x-3}\\).\n\nThe critical points are \\(x = 0\\), \\(x = 2\\), and \\(x = 3\\). Evaluating the sign changes around these points:\n- For \\(x < 0\\): Both \\(x(2-x)\\) and \\(x-3\\) are negative, so the expression is positive.\n- For \\(0 < x < 2\\): \\(x(2-x)\\) is positive and \\(x-3\\) is negative, so the expression is negative.\n- For \\(2 < x < 3\\): Both \\(x(2-x)\\) and \\(x-3\\) are negative, so the expression is positive.\n- For \\(x > 3\\): \\(x(2-x)\\) is negative and \\(x-3\\) is positive, so the expression is negative.\n\nThus, \\(y \\geq 0\\) for \\(x \\leq 0\\) or \\(2 < x < 3\\). Converting back to \\(n\\), this corresponds to \\(n \\leq 1\\) or \\(100 < n < 1000\\), but since \\(n \\neq 1000\\), we exclude \\(n = 1000\\).\n\nCounting the integers in these ranges:\n- For \\(n \\leq 1\\): the integer solutions are \\(n = 1\\).\n- For \\(100 < n < 1000\\): integer solutions are \\(n = 101, 102, \\ldots, 999\\), giving a count of \\(999 - 101 + 1 = 899\\) integers.\n\nSumming these possibilities, we find \\(1 + 899 = 900\\) integers for which the expression is real.",
  "answer": 900

Thus, the subset has k elements, and the smallest element is also k. This implies that the subset B can be represented as {k, a1, a2, ..., a(k-1)}, where k < a1 < a2 < ... < a(k-1). All these elements must be greater than k.\n\nFor a given k, the possible elements of B, besides k itself, are from the set {k+1, k+2, ..., 12}. The number of elements in this set is (12 - k). We need to choose (k-1) elements from these (12 - k) elements, which is done in C(12-k, k-1) ways, where C(n, k) is the binomial coefficient.\n\nTherefore, the number of subsets B for a particular k is C(12-k, k-1). We sum this for all possible k from 1 to 12 (since k = 0 would imply an empty set, which is not allowed).\n\nWe have:\n- For k = 1: C(11, 0) \n- For k = 2: C(10, 1)\n- For k = 3: C(9, 2)\n- For k = 4: C(8, 3)\n- For k = 5: C(7, 4)\n- For k = 6: C(6, 5)\n- For k = 7: C(5, 6)\n- For k = 8: C(4, 7)\n- For k = 9: C(3, 8)\n- For k = 10: C(2, 9)\n- For k = 11: C(1, 10)\n- For k = 12: C(0, 11)\n\nThe binomial coefficients simplify as follows:\nC(11, 0) = 1\nC(10, 1) = 10\nC(9, 2) = 36\nC(8, 3) = 56\nC(7, 4) = 35\nC(6, 5) = 6\nC(5, 6) = 0\nC(4, 7) = 0\nC(3, 8) = 0\nC(2, 9) = 0\nC(1, 10) = 0\nC(0, 11) = 0\n\nAdding these values gives the total number of valid subsets: 1 + 10 + 36 + 56 + 35 + 6 = 144.\n\nThus, the answer is 144.",

content: {"reasoning": "The given inequality is | |x| - 1 | + | |y| - 1 | ≤ 1. To solve this, we first need to understand the effect of the absolute values in the inequality.\n\nConsider the expression |a - 1|. This expression equals a - 1 if a ≥ 1, 1 - a if a ≤ -1, and 1 - |a| if -1 < a < 1. Applying this transformation to both |x| and |y| inside the inequality leads to different cases:\n\nCase 1: x ≥ 1 and y ≥ 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (x - 1) + (y - 1) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x + y ≤ 3.\n\nCase 2: x ≥ 1 and y ≤ -1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (x - 1) + (1 + y) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x + y ≤ 1.\n\nCase 3: x ≥ 1 and -1 < y < 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (x - 1) + (1 - |y|) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x ≤ 2.\n\nCase 4: -1 < x < 1 and y ≥ 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 - |x|) + (y - 1) ≤ 1, which simplifies to y ≤ 2.\n\nCase 5: -1 < x < 1 and -1 < y < 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 - |x|) + (1 - |y|) ≤ 1, which simplifies to |x| + |y| ≥ 1.\n\nCase 6: x ≤ -1 and y ≥ 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 + x) + (y - 1) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x + y ≤ 1.\n\nCase 7: x ≤ -1 and y ≤ -1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 + x) + (1 + y) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x + y ≤ -1.\n\nCase 8: x ≤ -1 and -1 < y < 1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 + x) + (1 - |y|) ≤ 1, which simplifies to x ≤ 0.\n\nCase 9: -1 < x < 1 and y ≤ -1.\nHere, the inequality becomes (1 - |x|) + (1 + y) ≤ 1, which simplifies to y ≤ 0.\n\nNext, we plot these inequalities on the coordinate plane. The region of interest is the intersection of these half-planes.\n\nThe resulting region is a diamond (rhombus) centered at the origin with vertices at (0, 0), (2, 0), (0, 2), and (-2, 0), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (-1, -1), and (1, 1). The region forms a square or rhombus with side length 2.\n\nThe side of the rhombus can be calculated using the distance formula or by observing that it's a unit distance from the origin to the points (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), and (0, -1). The area of a rhombus is given by the formula A = 0.5 * D1 * D2, where D1 and D2 are the lengths of the diagonals.\n\nIn our case, D1 = 4 and D2 = 4 (since they are along the lines y = x and y = -x, they go from (2, -2) to (-2, 2) and (2, 2) to (-2, -2)). Therefore, the area A = 0.5 * 4 * 4 = 8.", "answer": 8}

First, we consider the absolute value expressions: | |x| - 1| becomes 1 - |x| for -1 < x < 1, x - 1 for x ≥ 1, and 1 + x for x ≤ -1. Similarly, | |y| - 1| becomes 1 - |y| for -1 < y < 1, y - 1 for y ≥ 1, and 1 + y for y ≤ -1. By analyzing these expressions, the region forms a diamond shape centered at the origin, symmetric around both axes. The vertices of the diamond are (0, 0), (2, 0), (0, 2), (-2, 0), (0, -2), and other points within the region. The diagonals of this rhombus are each 4 units long, spanning from (-2, 2) to (2, -2) and from (-2, -2) to (2, 2). The area of a rhombus is given by the formula A = 0.5 * D1 * D2, where D1 and D2 are the lengths of the diagonals. Therefore, the area of the region is A = 0.5 * 4 * 4 = 8. Thus, the correct total area of the region is 8.",
